- ShatteredRimeMutation.cs:16 / StillHeartMutation.cs:29 / VerdigrisBloomMutation.cs:14 / HollowCoinMutation.cs:18 / SunderingWordMutation.cs:14 / BloodletterLedgerMutation.cs:15 → `: ConsumingRiteBase`
Only the **6 SM9 rites** inherit it. The base was written alongside them (git: `0ca70a2e feat(magic): SM9 — six rites you find rather than buy…`) and the 5 legacy rites were never migrated. **"The two invariants it enforces for everyone" enforces them for 6 of 11.** The port must either (a) port all 11 onto one `ConsumingRiteSkillBase`, finally making the docstring true, or (b) keep the 5 bespoke and repeat the claim's falsehood. Recommend (a).

## Declared surface (what a concrete rite must supply)
- `enum RiteShape { SingleTarget, Cone, Radius }` — :29
- `abstract string Command` :33 | `abstract string Element` :34 | `abstract RiteShape Shape` :35 | `abstract int Range` :36 | `abstract int Cooldown` :37
- `virtual int Slots => 2` :40 | `virtual int BaseDamage => 4` :44 | `virtual string DamageAttribute => ""` :46
- `protected abstract void ApplyPayoff(Entity target, ResonanceSystem.Result res, Zone zone)` :52-53 — per target, AFTER damage, **survivor-gated**
- `protected virtual void OnCastResolved(int totalMarks, Zone zone) { }` :69 — caster-side, once, **unconditional** (added because Bloodletter's heal vanished on kills; :55-67)
- `protected abstract string CastMessage(Entity caster, int targets, int marks)` :73
No other hooks exist. There is no pre-cast hook, no per-target-pre-damage hook, no rider hook, no cost hook. `res.Riders` is **never read by the base or by any of its 6 subclasses** (only StormAnvilMutation.cs:141-161 reads Riders at all).

## Lifecycle
- `Mutate(entity, level)` :77-86 → `base.Mutate` (BaseMutation.cs:66-70 → `BaseLevel = level; ChangeLevel(Level)`), then `ActivatedAbilityID = AddMyActivatedAbility(DisplayName, Command, "Rites", Shape==Radius ? SelfCentered : DirectionLine, Range)`. **Cone maps to DirectionLine** (:82-85).
- `Unmutate(entity)` :88-93 → `RemoveMyActivatedAbility(ActivatedAbilityID); ActivatedAbilityID = Guid.Empty; base.Unmutate(entity)`.
- `HandleEvent(GameEvent e)` :95-105 → `if (e.ID != Command) return true;` then Zone/dx/dy, `Cast(...)`, and on success `e.SetParameter("BlocksTurnAdvance", true); e.Handled = true; return false;`. On failure returns `true` (unhandled) → InputHandler prints "The rite fails to resolve." (InputHandler.cs:3157-3163) and does **not** end the turn.
- **The `"RNG"` event parameter is never read.** InputHandler sets it (InputHandler.cs:3138) and AIHelpers sets it (AIHelpers.cs:464); ConsumingRiteBase ignores it entirely. Any rite RNG (e.g. BleedingEffect's internal save rolls) is wall-clock seeded. The port gets `ctx.Rng` for free — thread it.

## Cast() — exact order of operations (:107-187)
1. `caster = ParentEntity`; null caster or null zone → `return false` **silently** (:109-110). No diag, no message.
2. `pos = zone.GetEntityPosition(caster)`; `pos.x < 0` → `return false` silently (:112-113). (Zone.cs:308-313 returns (-1,-1) when absent.)
3. **Direction guard** :115-119: `if (Shape != RiteShape.Radius && dx == 0 && dy == 0) { Reject(caster, "no_direction"); return false; }` — diag only, **no MessageLog**.
4. `GatherTargets(...)` :121. Empty → `Reject(caster, "no_target")` + `MessageLog.Add(caster.GetDisplayName() + "'s rite finds no mark.")` :125 → false. **This generic line replaced the 5 legacy rites' bespoke refusal messages** — all 6 subclasses share it.
5. **Ink AFTER targets** :129-137: `GrimoireInk.FindInked(caster)`; null → `Reject(caster, "no_ink")` + `"The pages are dry — no ink to spend."` → false.
6. `grimoire.TrySpend()` :138 — **return value discarded**. Safe only because FindInked already filtered on `HasCharge`; a TOCTOU-shaped latent bug, not a live one.
7. Per-target loop :140-160: `res = ResonanceSystem.Spend(target, Element, Slots, caster, zone)`; `marks += res.Consumed.Count`; `amount = (int)Math.Round(BaseDamage * res.Multiplier)`; `if (amount > 0)` build `Damage(amount)`, add `DamageAttribute` if non-empty, `CombatSystem.ApplyDamage(target, dmg, caster, zone)`; then `if (target.GetStatValue("Hitpoints", 0) > 0) ApplyPayoff(target, res, zone)`.
8. `OnCastResolved(marks, zone)` :164 — unconditional, even if everything died.
9. Diag :174-182: `Entity diagTarget = Shape == RiteShape.SingleTarget ? targets[0] : caster;` then `Diag.Record("spell", "RiteCast", caster, diagTarget, new { rite = Name, element = Element, shape = Shape.ToString(), targets = targets.Count, statusesConsumed = marks, inkLeft = grimoire.Charges })`.
10. `MessageLog.Add(CastMessage(caster, targets.Count, marks))` :184.
11. `CooldownMyActivatedAbility(ActivatedAbilityID, Cooldown)` :185 — **only on success**. (BaseMutation.cs:242-247 → ActivatedAbilitiesPart.cs:168-175, which sets BOTH `CooldownRemaining` and `MaxCooldown` at cast time.)
12. `return true`.

## GatherTargets (:189-209)
- `Radius` → `SpellTargeting.GetCreaturesInRadius(zone, x, y, Range, exclude: caster)` (SpellTargeting.cs:78-116). Chebyshev radius, `HasTag("Creature")` only, no LOS, no wall occlusion.
- `Cone` → `SpellTargeting.GetCreaturesInCone(zone, caster, x, y, dx, dy, Range)` (SpellTargeting.cs:237-311). Band width 2n-1, per-ray solid occlusion, creature-only.
- `default` (SingleTarget) → `RiteTargeting.FirstCreatureInLine(zone, caster, x, y, dx, dy, Range)` (RiteTargeting.cs:17-38). Stops at `cell.IsSolid()`, returns first `e.Tags.ContainsKey("Creature")`.
- **All three are creature-only.** No rite can hit a barrel, a tree, an ice wall or a pool. `SkillLine.Collect` (SkillLine.cs:90-125) DOES include `AbilityTargeting.IsElementalTarget` scenery — this is the concrete content of the migration plan's "fixes these by construction" (F11).

## Reject helper (:211-213)
`Diag.Record("spell", "RiteRejected", caster, caster, new { rite = Name, reason })`. Reasons emitted by the base: `no_direction`, `no_target`, `no_ink`. Nothing else. Channel `"spell"` is default-on (Diag.cs:119-120 — Assets/Scripts/Shared/Utilities/Diag.cs).

## The ink economy — everything around GrimoireInk.FindInked
- `GrimoireInk` (Assets/Scripts/Gameplay/Mutations/GrimoireInk.cs, 35 lines) is a static with ONE method: `FindInked(Entity caster)` :21-33. Walks `caster.GetPart<InventoryPart>().Objects` **in index order**, returns the FIRST `GrimoireChargePart` with `HasCharge`. Null inventory → null.
- **It is element-blind and rite-blind.** Casting Shattered Rime drains whichever inked grimoire happens to sit earliest in the pack — possibly the Storm Anvil book. Pinned as intentional-or-at-least-known by `ConsumingRiteHypothesisTests.Hypothesis_CastingOneRite_MayDrainAnotherRitesBook` (:223).
- `GrimoireChargePart` (Assets/Scripts/Gameplay/Magic/GrimoireChargePart.cs): `Charges = 10` :30, `MaxCharges = 10` :33, `ChargesPerVial = 5` :36, `HasCharge => Charges > 0` :38, `TrySpend()` :42-47 (decrement, false when empty), `Refill(int)` :52-59 (clamps, returns actual added). Public fields → reflection save path round-trips them.
- Every rite grimoire blueprint carries a `GrimoireCharge` part with 10/10/5 — e.g. StormAnvilGrimoire at Objects.json:25650-25668; all 11 identical. All inherit `RiteGrimoire` (Objects.json, `"Name": "RiteGrimoire"`, Inherits `Item`, has `Grimoire` + `Physics{Category:Books, Weight:1}` + `Commerce`).
- Acquisition: `GrimoirePart.DoRead` (Assets/Scripts/Gameplay/Mutations/GrimoirePart.cs:52-86) — reads `MutationClassName`, checks `mutations.HasMutation(...)`, calls `mutations.AddMutation(MutationClassName, MutationLevel)`. **The book is NOT consumed**, which is load-bearing: reading teaches, carrying-and-inked is what lets you cast. Port must add a `SkillClassName` branch (or repoint `MutationClassName` at SkillsPart.AddSkill) and update all 11 blueprints.
- 11 grimoire blueprints × loot placements in LootTables.json (SunderingWord:30, HollowCoin:108/374/419, ShatteredRime:172/316, BloodletterLedger:176/276, StillHeart:272/382, VerdigrisBloom:312/455/487, StormAnvil:713, HangingBolt:717, …). Pinned by `RiteTests.RiteGrimoires_AreStockedSomewhere` (:391).

## Mark / status consumption (ResonanceSystem — Assets/Scripts/Gameplay/Magic/ResonanceSystem.cs)
- `Spend(target, element, maxSlots, caster, zone)` :229-283 = `Preview` then remove. `Preview` :176-222 iterates `StatusEffectsPart.GetAllEffects()` (**the live list, insertion order** — StatusEffectsPart.cs:291) and for each effect computes `StatusKey` = class name minus trailing "Effect" (:154-162). No entry in the element table → `Declined` ("not_resonant_with_element"). Entry but `Consumed.Count >= maxSlots` → `Declined` ("no_slots_left").
- **Multiplier** :217-221: `1f + linear + QuadraticCoefficient * n * n`, QuadraticCoefficient = 0.25 (Resonance.json:2). n=0 → **exactly 1.0** — this is the mechanical truth behind invariant #2, and it lives in ResonanceSystem, not in the base.
- Removal :240-263: `effects.RemoveEffect(fx => StatusKey(fx) == wanted)` — StatusEffectsPart.cs:224-239 removes **the first match only** and stamps `CAUSE_EXTERNAL`. Emits `spell/ResonanceFired` per consumed, `spell/ResonanceDeclined` per declined (:265-280).
- `marks` counts `res.Consumed.Count`, i.e. what Preview *decided*, not what Spend *removed*. All shipped table entries have `Consume: true` so they agree today; a future `Consume:false` entry would silently over-count marks.
- Resonance tables (Assets/Resources/Content/Data/Resonance/Resonance.json): **Electric** Wet .75/Arc, Electrified .75/Stun, Frozen .5/Shatter. **Heat** Burning .75/Flare, Wet .75/Steam, Frozen .5/Thaw. **Cold** Wet .75/Encase, Frozen .75/Shatter. **Acid** Acidic .75/Corrode, Wet .5/Spread. **Any** Wet/Electrified/Frozen/Burning/Acidic all .4. Useful multipliers: matched n=1 → 2.00, n=2 → 3.50; Any n=1 → 1.65, n=2 → 2.80, n=3 → 4.45; Acid Acidic+Wet → 3.25.

## The two claimed invariants — VERIFIED, with caveats
**(1) "Ink is never spent on a cast that finds nothing."** REAL for the 6 subclasses: the targets check (:121-127) precedes `FindInked` (:131). It is also honoured longhand by all 5 legacy rites (StormAnvil 96-108, HangingBolt 86-94, RenderedSteam 75-83, Fulmination 95-103, ScaldingVeil 66-75 via `ResonanceSystem.Preview`). **Caveat:** "finds nothing" means *no targets*, not *no marks*. A cast that finds a target with zero resonant statuses spends ink for a 1.0-multiplier hit. That is deliberate but is NOT what the sentence says.
**(2) "A rite cast COLD is deliberately weak: the multiplier from consuming nothing is 1.0."** REAL, but enforced in ResonanceSystem.cs:220, not here. The base's own contribution is the small `BaseDamage` default (4, :44). It cannot stop a subclass declaring `BaseDamage => 40`.

## Exact BaseMutation dependencies (the full port specification)
| Used from BaseMutation | Site | BaseSkillPart replacement |
|---|---|---|
| `ActivatedAbilityID` (Guid field, BaseMutation.cs:38) | :80, :90, :185 | `BaseSkillPart.ActivatedAbilityID` (BaseSkillPart.cs:267) — same name, already serialized |
| `AddMyActivatedAbility(name, cmd, class, mode, range)` (BaseMutation.cs:210-227) | :80-85 | Override `DeclareActivatedAbility(Entity)` → `new ActivatedAbilitySpec { DisplayName, Command, Class = "Rites", TargetingMode, Range, Cooldown }`. SkillsPart.cs:122-139 registers it and sets `MaxCooldown` at ADD time (vs mutation path which sets MaxCooldown at first CAST). Keep `Class = "Rites"` — `ActivatedAbility.Class` is a live UI group key (AbilityManagerStateBuilder.cs:61). |
| `RemoveMyActivatedAbility(id)` (BaseMutation.cs:232-237) | :90 | **Delete.** SkillsPart.RemoveSkill (SkillsPart.cs:198-208) already deregisters + clears the Guid. |
| `CooldownMyActivatedAbility(id, turns)` (BaseMutation.cs:242-247) | :185 | **⚠ NO SAFE EQUIVALENT — see hazard note.** |
| `Mutate(Entity, int)` / `Unmutate(Entity)` | :77, :88 | `AddSkill(Entity) → bool` / `RemoveSkill(Entity) → bool` (BaseSkillPart.cs:94, :107) |
| `base.Mutate` → `BaseLevel`/`ChangeLevel`/`CalcLevel`/`GetMutationCap`/`RapidLevel` (BaseMutation.cs:18-201) | :79 | **All dead weight.** No rite reads `Level`. Drop entirely — confirms plan F3. |
| `abstract string DisplayName` (BaseMutation.cs:48) | :81, and every subclass | `BaseSkillPart.DisplayName` is `virtual` with a SkillRegistry lookup fallback (BaseSkillPart.cs:70-83). Rites can keep hardcoded overrides, or drop them and author the names in Rites.json. |
| `abstract string MutationType` (BaseMutation.cs:43) | every subclass returns `"Rite"` | **Delete.** `grep MutationType` outside Assets/Scripts/Gameplay/Mutations/ → zero consumers. |
| `Part.Name` / `Part.ParentEntity` / `Part.HandleEvent` | :97-104, :109, and `Diag` payload `rite = Name` | Unchanged — `BaseSkillPart : Part`. Note skills conventionally do `Name => nameof(Class)`; rites do `Name => "ShatteredRime"`. **Preserve the short names or the `rite=` diag payload value changes** and existing diag queries break. |
| `HandleEvent` dispatch | :95-105 | **Delete the whole override.** SkillsPart.HandleEvent (SkillsPart.cs:380-420) already claims `Command*` events, reads Zone/RNG/dx/dy correctly, and routes to `OnCommand(SkillEventContext)`. |

## ⚠ THE THREE PORT HAZARDS (must be designed for, not discovered)
**H1 — Cooldown is applied unconditionally by the skill dispatcher.** SkillsPart.cs:284: `ability.CooldownRemaining = ability.MaxCooldown;` runs immediately after `skill.OnCommand(ctx)` with no success signal. A ported rite that refuses for `no_target` / `no_ink` / `no_direction` would eat its full 25-50 turn cooldown. The comment at SkillsPart.cs:280-283 ("OnCommand … can manually set ability.CooldownRemaining = 0 before returning") is **factually wrong** — line 284 overwrites it. Fix options: (a) give `OnCommand` a bool return or an `ctx.SuppressCooldown` flag and honour it at :284; (b) have the rite zero `ability.MaxCooldown` before returning (destructive, rejected); (c) apply cooldown from inside `OnCommand` and set `MaxCooldown = 0` at registration (breaks the hotbar cooldown display). **Recommend (a) — one dispatcher change, benefits every skill.**
**H2 — A refusal now costs a turn.** Mutation path: `Cast` false → `HandleEvent` returns true → `cmd.Handled == false` → InputHandler.cs:3157-3163 prints "The rite fails to resolve." and does NOT call `EndTurnAndProcess`. Skill path: `TryRouteSkillCommand` returns true regardless of what `OnCommand` did (SkillsPart.cs:287) → `e.Handled = true` → InputHandler falls through to `EndTurnAndProcess()` at :3172. Net regression: a refused rite costs a turn **and** a cooldown. Same fix as H1.
**H3 — `BlocksTurnAdvance` is not set by the skill path.** ConsumingRiteBase.cs:102 sets it; SkillsPart.HandleEvent never does. Effect: the mutation path waits in `InputState.WaitingForFxResolution` for blocking FX before ending the turn (InputHandler.cs:3164-3170, 3120-3129); the skill path ends the turn immediately. Probably acceptable, but it is a real behaviour change and no other skill currently sets the flag.

## Test surface that must survive the port
`Assets/Tests/EditMode/Gameplay/Magic/` — ConsumingRiteTests.cs (810 lines), ConsumingRiteAdversarialTests.cs (460), ConsumingRiteHypothesisTests.cs (313), RiteTests.cs (401), FulminationTests.cs (248), ResonanceSystemTests.cs (255). **Every one of them calls `rite.Cast(zone, dx, dy)` directly** — zero tests fire a GameEvent through `HandleEvent`. That is exactly why F17 (the dead-direction bug) was invisible for its entire shipped life. The port MUST add at least one end-to-end test that goes `GameEvent.New(command).SetParameter("DirectionX", 1)` → `entity.FireEvent(...)` → assert the cast happened, or the same class of bug recurs. Pinned diag contracts: `spell/RiteCast` payload must contain `statusesConsumed` and `inkLeft` (ConsumingRiteAdversarialTests.cs:369-375); a refusal must emit no `RiteCast` (:378-392).


## StormAnvilMutation

FILE: Assets/Scripts/Gameplay/Mutations/StormAnvilMutation.cs (184 lines). **Extends `BaseMutation` directly (:30) — NOT ConsumingRiteBase.**

- **Consumes:** Electric table, `SLOTS = 2` (:42) → Wet (.75/Arc), Electrified (.75/Stun), Frozen (.5/Shatter).
- **Does:** radius-2 nova. Damage `(int)Math.Round(BASE_DAMAGE * res.Multiplier)` with `BASE_DAMAGE = 4` (:46, :131), attributes `Electric` + `Lightning` (:133-134). n=2 → 4×3.5 = **14**.
- **The ONLY rite that reads `res.Riders`** (:141-161): `"Stun"` → `StunnedEffect(duration: STUN_RIDER_TURNS=3)` (:49, :146-148); `"Arc"` → `ElectrifiedEffect(charge: 1.0f)` re-applied to the target (:154-155 — spending Wet leaves the target primed again, deliberate); `"Shatter"` → `BrokenEffect()` (:158).
- **Targeting:** `AbilityTargetingMode.SelfCentered`, `RADIUS = 2` (:33, :59-60). `HandleEvent` reads `Zone` and **`Cell SourceCell`** (:74-75) — object-typed params, so `GetParameter<Cell>` works.
- **Ink:** 1 charge via `GrimoireInk.FindInked` (:108), after the target check (:94-106). Ordering invariant honoured longhand.
- **Overridden hooks:** `Name`/`MutationType`("Rite")/`DisplayName` (:51-53), `Mutate` (:55-61), `Unmutate` (:63-68), `HandleEvent` (:70-81), plus its own public `Cast(Zone, Cell)` (:83) — **a different signature from every ConsumingRiteBase rite's `Cast(Zone,int,int)`**.
- **Messages:** no-target → `"<name> holds the rite — there is nothing to strike."` (:103-104). No-ink → `"The pages are dry — <name> has no ink to spend."` (:113-114) — note this one names the caster; the base's version does not. Success → `"<name> brings down the storm anvil" + (marks>0 ? ", spending N mark(s)!" : " — but nothing was primed to spend.")` (:174-178).
- **Diag:** `spell/RiteRejected` reasons `no_zone_or_cell` (:89-91, unique to this rite), `no_target` (:101), `no_ink` (:111). `spell/RiteCast` filed under **caster** with `{rite, element, targets, statusesConsumed, inkLeft}` (:164-172) — **no `shape` field** (the base adds one).
- **BROKEN by F17? NO.** SelfCentered → InputHandler.cs:3052-3055 and :1716-1719 call `ResolveAbilityCommand(ability, playerCell, 0, 0, null)` directly and the rite reads `SourceCell`, not DirectionX/Y. AI path also fine (AIHelpers.cs:474-476 sets SourceCell for SelfCentered).
- **Quirks:** (1) Applies damage unconditionally — no `amount > 0` guard, harmless since multiplier ≥ 1. (2) Never checks the `"RNG"` param. (3) Cooldown 25 (:34), tooltip agrees ("Nova 2 • CD 25", GrimoireTooltipData.cs:144).
- **Port target:** `Rites_StormAnvil : ConsumingRiteSkillBase`, `Shape = Radius`, `Range = 2`, `Cooldown = 25`, `Element = "Electric"`, `Slots = 2`, `BaseDamage = 4`, `DamageAttribute` — ⚠ the base supports only ONE attribute (`ConsumingRiteBase.cs:151-152`) but this rite adds two (`Electric` + `Lightning`). **The base needs `virtual string[] DamageAttributes` or a `virtual void DecorateDamage(Damage)` hook, or Storm Anvil loses its `Lightning` tag.** Riders need a new hook too: `protected virtual void ApplyRider(Entity target, string rider, Zone zone)` called from the survivor block, since no existing hook sees `res.Riders`.


## HangingBoltMutation

FILE: Assets/Scripts/Gameplay/Mutations/HangingBoltMutation.cs (134 lines). **Extends `BaseMutation` directly (:21).**

- **Consumes:** Electric table, `SLOTS = 2` (:27).
- **Does:** the marks become **paralysis, not damage**. `turns = res.Consumed.Count * PARALYSIS_PER_MARK` with `PARALYSIS_PER_MARK = 2` (:36, :111); `if (hp > 0 && turns > 0) target.ApplyEffect(new ParalyzedEffect(turns), caster, zone)` (:112-113). ParalyzedEffect ctor `(int duration = 3)`.
- **⚠ MAJOR QUIRK — the damage IGNORES the resonance multiplier.** `:106` is `var dmg = new Damage(BASE_DAMAGE);` — flat **2** (`BASE_DAMAGE = 2`, :30), never multiplied. Attributes `Electric` + `Lightning` (:107-108). Every other rite scales damage by `res.Multiplier`. Deliberate per the docstring ("Token damage — this rite is control, not a kill") but it means the port cannot use the base's `BaseDamage * Multiplier` path unmodified: it needs `virtual bool ScaleDamageByResonance => true` (false here), or `BaseDamage` stays 2 and a `virtual float DamageMultiplier(res)` returns 1.
- **Targeting:** `AbilityTargetingMode.DirectionLine`, `RANGE = 6` (:24, :46-47). `RiteTargeting.FirstCreatureInLine` (:85) — nearest creature, stops at solid.
- **Ink:** 1 charge (:94), after the target check (:85-92).
- **Overridden hooks:** `Name`/`MutationType`/`DisplayName` (:38-40), `Mutate` (:42-48), `Unmutate` (:50-55), `HandleEvent` (:57-69), `Cast(Zone,int,int)` (:71).
- **Messages:** no-target → `"<name> finds nothing to pin."` (:90). No-ink → `"The pages are dry — no ink to spend."` (:99). Success → `"<name> pins <target> under a hanging bolt for N turns!"` or, at 0 marks, `"<name>'s hanging bolt fizzles — <target> carried nothing to spend."` (:124-128). No-direction → **diag only, no message** (:77-79).
- **Diag:** `RiteRejected` reasons `no_zone` / `no_direction` (ternary, :78) and `no_target` (:88-89), `no_ink` (:97-98). `RiteCast` filed under **target** (:115) with `{rite, element, statusesConsumed, paralysisTurns, inkLeft}` — **`paralysisTurns` is a bespoke payload field the generic base cannot emit.** Port needs `protected virtual object ExtraDiagPayload()` or the field is lost.
- **⚡ BROKEN by F17? YES — dead from the keybind.** `HangingBoltMutation.cs:62-63` uses `e.GetParameter<int>("DirectionX"/"DirectionY")`, which reads only `GameEvent.Parameters` (GameEvent.cs:154-159). `InputHandler.cs:3140-3141` writes them through `SetParameter(string,int)`, which stores in `GameEvent.IntParameters` (GameEvent.cs:124-128). Result: always (0,0) → `:75` guard trips → `RiteRejected{reason:"no_direction"}` → false → "The rite fails to resolve." AI path identically dead (AIHelpers.cs:471-472, same int overload).
- **Cooldown:** 30 (:25). Tooltip agrees ("Line 6 • spends 2 statuses • CD 30", GrimoireTooltipData.cs:151).
- **Port target:** `Shape = SingleTarget`, `Range = 6`, `Cooldown = 30`, `Slots = 2`, `Element = "Electric"`. Needs the no-scaling hook, the two-attribute hook, and the extra-diag hook. Fixed by construction: reading `ctx.DirectionX` (populated via `SkillsPart.cs:404` `GetIntParameter`) makes the ability live again.


## RenderedSteamMutation

FILE: Assets/Scripts/Gameplay/Mutations/RenderedSteamMutation.cs (135 lines). **Extends `BaseMutation` directly (:23).**

- **Consumes:** Heat table, `SLOTS = 2` (:29) → Burning (.75/Flare), Wet (.75/Steam), Frozen (.5/Thaw).
- **Does:** radius-2 nova whose whole point is consuming **Wet AND Burning off the same body**. `bool pair = res.Consumed.Contains("Wet") && res.Consumed.Contains("Burning")` (:99); `float mult = res.Multiplier + (pair ? PAIR_BONUS : 0f)` — **additive 1.5, not multiplicative** (`PAIR_BONUS = 1.5f`, :34, :100). Damage `(int)Math.Round(BASE_DAMAGE * mult)`, `BASE_DAMAGE = 5` (:30, :103), attributes `Fire` + `Heat` (:104-105). Pair case: mult = 3.5 + 1.5 = 5.0 → **25 damage**.
- **Rider:** only on a real pair, `ConfusedEffect(CONFUSE_TURNS=3)` on survivors (:36, :113-114). Never reads `res.Riders`.
- **Targeting:** `SelfCentered`, `RADIUS = 2` (:26, :46-47); reads `Cell SourceCell` (:61).
- **Ink:** 1 charge (:83), after the target check (:73-81).
- **Overridden hooks:** `Name`/`MutationType`/`DisplayName` (:38-40), `Mutate` (:42-48), `Unmutate` (:50-55), `HandleEvent` (:57-66), `Cast(Zone, Cell)` (:68).
- **Messages:** no-target → `"<name> holds the rite — nothing to render."` (:79). No-ink → `"The pages are dry — no ink to spend."` (:88). Success → `"<name>'s rite renders N body/ies to scalding steam!"` or `"<name>'s rite hisses — nothing was both soaked and burning."` (:126-129). **Note the pluralisation bug at :128: `"body" + (detonations == 1 ? "" : "ies")` yields "bodyies".**
- **Diag:** `RiteRejected` `no_target` (:77-78), `no_ink` (:86-87). **No reject record at all for null zone/cell — `:71` returns false silently**, unlike StormAnvil's `no_zone_or_cell`. This is one of the "four of them are already subtly different" cases the base's docstring alludes to. `RiteCast` filed under **caster** (:117) with `{rite, element, targets, steamDetonations, inkLeft}` — `steamDetonations` is another bespoke payload field, and note it does **not** emit `statusesConsumed`, breaking the payload convention every other rite follows.
- **BROKEN by F17? NO.** SelfCentered + `SourceCell`.
- **⚠ DOC DRIFT:** `COOLDOWN = 35` (:27) but GrimoireTooltipData.cs:158 says "CD 30". Combined with ScaldingVeil's inverse error, **the two tooltip cooldowns are swapped.**
- **Port target:** `Shape = Radius`, `Range = 2`, `Cooldown = 35`, `Element = "Heat"`, `Slots = 2`, `BaseDamage = 5`. Needs: two-attribute damage hook; a **pre-damage multiplier hook** (`protected virtual float AdjustMultiplier(Entity target, ResonanceSystem.Result res, float baseMult)`) because PAIR_BONUS must land BEFORE damage — `ApplyPayoff` fires after; a per-cast counter surfaced to `CastMessage` (the base only passes `targets` and `marks`, so `detonations` has nowhere to live — either add `protected virtual object ExtraDiagPayload()` + an instance field, or widen `CastMessage`).


## ScaldingVeilMutation

FILE: Assets/Scripts/Gameplay/Mutations/ScaldingVeilMutation.cs (105 lines). **Extends `BaseMutation` directly (:20). The structural outlier of the eleven.**

- **Consumes:** Heat table, `SLOTS = 1` (:25) — **off the CASTER, not a target**. `ResonanceSystem.Spend(caster, ELEMENT, SLOTS, caster, zone)` (:86).
- **Does:** no damage, no targets, no target list at all. Applies `ScaldingVeilEffect(VEIL_DURATION=8, VEIL_SCALD=3, VEIL_CONFUSE=2)` to the caster (:27-29, :88-90). The effect (Assets/Scripts/Gameplay/Effects/Concrete/ScaldingVeilEffect.cs) is a retaliation aura on `OnTakeDamage` (:52-...): reads `e.GetParameter<Entity>("Source")`, no-ops on null source or self-damage (:56-57), else deals `Damage(Scald)` with `Fire` + confusion back at the attacker.
- **Precondition, not a target check:** `ResonanceSystem.Preview(caster, ELEMENT, SLOTS)` and `if (!preview.Consumed.Contains("Wet"))` → reject (:66-73). **This is the only rite that gates on a specific status name.** Note Heat's table also lists Burning (.75) and Frozen (.5) — with `Slots = 1` and insertion-ordered iteration, a caster who is Burning **and** Wet may have Preview pick Burning first, fail the `Contains("Wet")` gate, and refuse despite being soaked. Untested latent bug.
- **Targeting:** `SelfCentered`, range **0** (:39-40) — note `ActivatedAbilitiesPart.AddAbility` clamps `range < 1 ? 1 : range` (ActivatedAbilitiesPart.cs:61), so the stored Range is 1. Harmless for SelfCentered.
- **Ink:** 1 charge (:75), after the wetness check (:66-73). Invariant honoured with a different mechanism from every other rite.
- **Overridden hooks:** `Name`/`MutationType`/`DisplayName` (:31-33), `Mutate` (:35-41), `Unmutate` (:43-48), `HandleEvent` (:50-57 — reads ONLY `Zone`), `Cast(Zone)` (:59) — **a third distinct Cast signature.**
- **Messages:** dry → `"<name> is dry — there is nothing to boil."` (:71). No-ink → `"The pages are dry — no ink to spend."` (:80). **No success message at all** — the only player feedback on a successful cast is `ScaldingVeilEffect.OnApply`'s `"<name> is wreathed in scalding steam!"` (ScaldingVeilEffect.cs:40-41). The base's `CastMessage` is abstract, so the port is forced to invent one or return the OnApply line and double-log it.
- **Diag:** `RiteRejected` `caster_not_wet` (:69-70, unique), `no_ink` (:78-79). `RiteCast` under caster (:92) with `{rite, element, statusesConsumed, selfTargeted:true, inkLeft}`.
- **BROKEN by F17? NO.** SelfCentered, never reads DirectionX/Y at all.
- **⚠ DOC DRIFT:** `COOLDOWN = 30` (:23) vs GrimoireTooltipData.cs:165 "CD 35" — swapped with Rendered Steam's.
- **Port target — HARDEST OF THE ELEVEN.** It does not fit `ConsumingRiteBase`'s spine at any point: no target gathering, no damage, no per-target loop, a caster-as-target Spend, and a Preview-based precondition. Two honest options: (a) add `RiteShape.SelfOnly` to the enum, and in `Cast` branch to `targets = new List<Entity>{ caster }` with `virtual bool RequirePreview` + `virtual string RequiredStatus` gates; (b) port it as a plain `BaseSkillPart` that reuses `GrimoireInk` + `ResonanceSystem` directly and skips the rite base. **(b) is smaller and more honest** — the docstring's spine genuinely does not describe this rite.


## FulminationMutation

FILE: Assets/Scripts/Gameplay/Mutations/FulminationMutation.cs (150 lines). **Extends `BaseMutation` directly (:28).** The PALIMPSEST-P5 proof-of-concept rite.

- **Consumes:** Electric table, `SLOTS = 1` (:37) — "a detonator for a single setup, not a vacuum".
- **Does:** small direct hit, then **writes charge to the tile and stops**. `ZoneTileStateSystem.AddCharge(zone, tpos.x, tpos.y, WRITTEN_CHARGE=2, caster, Name)` then `ZoneTileStateSystem.ResolveAfterAbility(zone, caster)` (:46, :126-132; Assets/Scripts/Gameplay/World/Map/ZoneTileStateSystem.cs:167-173 and :87-91). What happens next — electrified water, charge crossing a metal grate, or nothing on dry stone — is decided entirely by the tile layer. Pinned by FulminationTests.cs:101/116/135/154.
- **⚠ QUIRK — bespoke damage formula.** `:116`: `new Damage(BASE_DAMAGE + (int)Math.Round(BASE_DAMAGE * (res.Multiplier - 1f)))` with `BASE_DAMAGE = 3` (:41). This is *not* `round(Base × Mult)` — it rounds the *bonus* separately, so it can differ by 1 from the base class formula at half-integer multipliers. n=0 → 3; n=1 (Wet or Electrified, ×2.0) → 3 + 3 = 6. Attributes `Electric` + `Lightning` (:117-118).
- **No rider.** Never reads `res.Riders`. Damage is applied unconditionally (no survivor gate) and the tile write happens even if the target died (`tpos` is read after ApplyDamage — if the corpse was removed from the zone, `tpos.x < 0` and the charge is silently not written, :126-127).
- **Targeting:** `DirectionLine`, `RANGE = 5` (:31, :56-57), `RiteTargeting.FirstCreatureInLine` (:93-94).
- **Ink:** 1 charge (:103), after the target check (:93-101).
- **Overridden hooks:** `Name`/`MutationType`/`DisplayName` (:48-50 — DisplayName is `"Fulmination"`, the only rite not named "Rite of the …"), `Mutate` (:52-58), `Unmutate` (:60-65), `HandleEvent` (:67-77), `Cast(Zone,int,int)` (:79).
- **Messages:** no-target → `"<name>'s fulmination finds no mark."` (:99). No-ink → `"The pages are dry — no ink to spend."` (:108). Success → `"<name>'s fulmination cracks into <target>, and the charge goes looking for somewhere to run."` (:143-144) — **unconditional; there is no cold-cast variant**, unlike every other rite.
- **Diag:** `RiteRejected` `no_direction` (:85-86, no message), `no_target` (:97-98), `no_ink` (:105-106). `RiteCast` filed under **target** (:134) with `{rite, element, statusesConsumed, wroteCharge, inkLeft}` — `wroteCharge` is bespoke.
- **⚡ BROKEN by F17? YES.** `FulminationMutation.cs:71-72` uses `e.GetParameter<int>("DirectionX"/"DirectionY")` → always 0 → `:83` guard → `no_direction` → "The rite fails to resolve." The audit names it explicitly (SYSTEMS-AUDIT-2026-08.md:123). AI path dead too. **Note: FulminationTests.cs never fires the event — it calls `Cast(zone, 1, 0)` directly, which is why 248 lines of tests are green on a dead ability.**
- **Cooldown:** 25 (:32); tooltip agrees (GrimoireTooltipData.cs:172).
- **Port target:** `Shape = SingleTarget`, `Range = 5`, `Cooldown = 25`, `Slots = 1`, `Element = "Electric"`, `BaseDamage = 3`. Needs: two-attribute hook; either accept the base's `round(Base×Mult)` formula (a 0-1 point change, worth taking — document it as a divergence) or a `virtual int ComputeDamage(res)` hook; and a post-damage-non-survivor-gated hook, because the tile write must happen **even when the target dies** — `ApplyPayoff` is survivor-gated (ConsumingRiteBase.cs:158-159) so putting the AddCharge there would silently break Fulmination the same way it broke Bloodletter's heal. Use `OnCastResolved`, or add `protected virtual void OnTargetResolved(Entity target, ResonanceSystem.Result res, Zone zone)` that fires unconditionally.


## ShatteredRimeMutation

FILE: Assets/Scripts/Gameplay/Mutations/ShatteredRimeMutation.cs (56 lines). **Extends `ConsumingRiteBase` (:16).** The whole file is declarations + two hook overrides — the shape the port should aim for.

- **Consumes:** `Element => "Cold"` (:23), `Slots => 2` (:27) → Wet (.75/Encase), Frozen (.75/Shatter). Both at .75, so n=2 → ×3.50.
- **Does:** cone of shatter damage. `BaseDamage => 6` (:28) — the highest of the six SM9 rites. n=0 → 6; n=1 → 12; n=2 → **21**.
- **`DamageAttribute => ""` — untyped ON PURPOSE** (:30-37). The docstring cites CombatSystem.cs:1181 (resistance 100 zeroes the hit) and reasons that tagging it Cold would let an ice creature absorb its own shattering. Pinned by ConsumingRiteTests.ShatteredRime_BreaksIceCreaturesToo (:334).
- **Targeting:** `Shape => RiteShape.Cone` (:24), `Range => 3` (:25). Base maps Cone → `AbilityTargetingMode.DirectionLine` (ConsumingRiteBase.cs:82-85) and `SpellTargeting.GetCreaturesInCone` (:198-200). Cone covers 1+3+5 = 9 cells at length 3, with per-ray solid occlusion (SpellTargeting.cs:255-300).
- **Ink:** 1 charge, via the base's spine.
- **Overridden hooks:** exactly two — `ApplyPayoff` (:39-47) and `CastMessage` (:49-54). Does **not** override `OnCastResolved`.
- **ApplyPayoff:** `if (res.Consumed.Count > 0) target.ApplyEffect(new ShatterArmorEffect(), ParentEntity, zone)` — `ShatterArmorEffect(int duration = 4)`. Note it applies ONE ShatterArmor regardless of marks, despite the comment "Every mark spent adds a shard" (:43) — **docstring-vs-impl drift: the comment claims per-mark scaling the code does not do.** Counter-checked cold by ConsumingRiteTests.ShatteredRime_ShattersArmour_AndOnlyWhenFed (:545).
- **Messages:** success/cold → `"<name>'s rime shatters across N target(s)" + (marks>0 ? ", spending N mark(s)!" : " — nothing was brittle.")` (:51-53). No-target and no-ink messages come from the base (generic `"…'s rite finds no mark."` / `"The pages are dry — no ink to spend."`).
- **Diag:** base-supplied. `RiteCast` filed under **caster** (Shape != SingleTarget, ConsumingRiteBase.cs:174).
- **⚡ BROKEN by F17? YES.** Cone is a directional shape, so ConsumingRiteBase.cs:99-100's `GetParameter<int>` returns (0,0) and the guard at :115 trips (`Shape != Radius && dx==0 && dy==0`) → `RiteRejected{no_direction}`, no message, "The rite fails to resolve."
- **Cooldown:** 40 (:26); tooltip agrees (GrimoireTooltipData.cs:179).
- **Port target:** trivially mechanical — `Rites_ShatteredRime : ConsumingRiteSkillBase` with the same seven property overrides plus the same two hook bodies. Zero new base hooks required. Port this one FIRST as the shape-proof.


## StillHeartMutation

FILE: Assets/Scripts/Gameplay/Mutations/StillHeartMutation.cs (68 lines). **Extends `ConsumingRiteBase` (:29).**

- **Consumes:** `Element => "Cold"` (:36), `Slots => 2` (:46, with a docstring explaining the 2 exists so duration can scale).
- **Does:** the only rite whose payoff is **removal**. `AsleepByGasEffect(duration: 8 * res.Consumed.Count)` on survivors with marks > 0 (:55-58). `AsleepByGasEffect(int duration = 5)` — blocks action, wakes on damage, buffs nothing; re-application takes `max(Duration, other.Duration)` (AsleepByGasEffect.cs:65).
- **⚠ HISTORICAL BUG, ALREADY FIXED — do not re-introduce.** It shipped applying `HibernatingEffect`, which is a SELF-buff: heals 5%/turn, forces Heat AND Cold resistance to 100, no wake-on-damage. Cast at an elite it healed the elite and made it immune to this rite's own element. Fixed in `9f82c862 fix(magic): SM9 cold-eye — Still Heart was healing the elite it was meant to remove`. Counter-checked by ConsumingRiteTests.StillHeart_DoesNotBuffTheEnemyItPutsToSleep (:393) and StillHeart_SleeperWakesWhenStruck (:417). **The port must carry AsleepByGasEffect, not HibernatingEffect.**
- **Damage:** `BaseDamage => 2` (:47), deliberately tiny — "hitting it awake would defeat the entire purpose". `DamageAttribute => "Cold"` (:48). n=2 → round(2×3.5) = **7**. There is a live design tension here the port should note: 7 Cold damage lands BEFORE the sleep (ConsumingRiteBase.cs:147-159), and the sleep only lands if the target survived it.
- **Targeting:** `Shape => SingleTarget` (:37), `Range => 5` (:38) → base maps to `DirectionLine` + `RiteTargeting.FirstCreatureInLine`.
- **Ink:** 1 charge via the base.
- **Overridden hooks:** `ApplyPayoff` (:50-59), `CastMessage` (:61-66). No `OnCastResolved`.
- **Messages:** marks>0 → `"<name> stills a heart — it will not wake until struck."`; cold → `"<name>'s rite finds a heart already beating too fast to still."` (:63-65). Ignores the `targets` parameter entirely (single-target, so always 1).
- **Diag:** base-supplied; `RiteCast` filed under the **victim** (`Shape == SingleTarget` → ConsumingRiteBase.cs:174, pinned by ConsumingRiteTests.SingleTargetRite_FilesItsDiagUnderTheVictim :457).
- **⚡ BROKEN by F17? YES.** SingleTarget is directional → base guard trips at (0,0).
- **Cooldown:** 45 (:39); tooltip agrees (GrimoireTooltipData.cs:186).
- **Port target:** mechanical, no new base hooks. Note the migration plan (Docs/MUTATIONS-TO-SKILLS-MIGRATION.md:76) says "StillHeart is a Rite and ports" — that line disambiguates it from the unrelated dead passive of the same name. `ConsumingRiteHypothesisTests.Hypothesis_ReCastingStillHeart_ExtendsTheSleepRatherThanWastingTheCharge` (:251) pins the re-cast contract.


## VerdigrisBloomMutation

FILE: Assets/Scripts/Gameplay/Mutations/VerdigrisBloomMutation.cs (52 lines). **Extends `ConsumingRiteBase` (:14).**

- **Consumes:** `Element => "Acid"` (:21), `Slots => 2` (:24) → Acidic (.75/Corrode), Wet (.5/Spread). n=2 → ×3.25 (the weakest matched two-mark payout in the game).
- **Does:** support rite — radius armour-strip. `BaseDamage => 5` (:26), `DamageAttribute => "Acid"` (:27). n=0 → 5; n=1 (Acidic) → 10; n=2 → round(5×3.25) = **16**.
- **ApplyPayoff (:29-43):** early-returns at `res.Consumed.Count == 0`; then `ShatterArmorEffect()` (:34) **and** `AcidicEffect(corrosion: 0.6f)` (:42). The second is a deliberate **re-seed of the status the rite may have just eaten** — §7.4 wanted Wet to SPREAD acid across the radius, and re-seeding every caught target is the spread. Guarded against pumping by the 40-turn cooldown + the weaker 0.6 corrosion (comment :36-41). `AcidicEffect(float corrosion = 1.0f)` has `Duration = DURATION_INDEFINITE`. Counter-checked cold by ConsumingRiteTests.VerdigrisBloom_ReSeedsAcid_AndOnlyWhenFed (:567).
- **Targeting:** `Shape => RiteShape.Radius` (:22), `Range => 2` (:23). Base maps Radius → `AbilityTargetingMode.SelfCentered` (ConsumingRiteBase.cs:82-84) and `SpellTargeting.GetCreaturesInRadius(zone, casterX, casterY, 2, exclude: caster)` (:194-196). **Chebyshev, caster-centred, no LOS/occlusion** — it will hit through walls, unlike the Cone.
- **Ink:** 1 charge via the base.
- **Overridden hooks:** `ApplyPayoff` (:29-43), `CastMessage` (:45-50). No `OnCastResolved`.
- **Messages:** `"<name>'s verdigris blooms across N target(s)" + (marks>0 ? ", eating N ward(s) away!" : ".")` (:47-49).
- **Diag:** base-supplied; `RiteCast` filed under **caster** (Radius). Pinned by ConsumingRiteTests.RadiusRite_StillFilesItsDiagUnderTheCaster (:477).
- **✅ BROKEN by F17? NO — one of only two ConsumingRiteBase rites that still works from the keybind.** Two independent reasons: (1) `AbilityTargetingMode.SelfCentered` makes InputHandler call `ResolveAbilityCommand(ability, playerCell, 0, 0, null)` without ever entering AwaitingDirection (InputHandler.cs:3052-3055, :1716-1719); (2) even if dx/dy arrived as (0,0), the base's guard is `Shape != RiteShape.Radius && dx == 0 && dy == 0` (ConsumingRiteBase.cs:115) so Radius is explicitly exempt, and `GatherTargets` uses the caster's own position (:194-196), not the direction. Confirmed also by ConsumingRiteAdversarialTests.Adversarial_RadiusRiteAcceptsZeroDirection (:110).
- **Cooldown:** 40 (:24 — note `Cooldown => 40` at :24 and `Slots => 2` at :24-25 region; exact lines: Range :23, Cooldown :24, Slots :25, BaseDamage :26). Tooltip agrees (GrimoireTooltipData.cs:193).
- **Quirk:** it is still fully resisted by acid immunity (ConsumingRiteTests.VerdigrisBloom_IsStillResistedByAcidImmunity :353) — the deliberate contrast with Hollow Coin's untyped burst.
- **Port target:** mechanical, no new base hooks.


## HollowCoinMutation

FILE: Assets/Scripts/Gameplay/Mutations/HollowCoinMutation.cs (60 lines). **Extends `ConsumingRiteBase` (:18).**

- **Consumes:** `Element => "Any"` (:35) — the wildcard table (Resonance.json:84-118): Wet / Electrified / Frozen / Burning / Acidic, **all at Mult 0.4**. `Slots => 3` (:39), the only 3-slot rite. Multipliers: n=1 → 1.65, n=2 → 2.80, n=3 → **4.45**.
- **Does:** flat untyped burst. `BaseDamage => 3` (:40), `DamageAttribute => ""` (:41) — untyped on purpose so nothing resists it (pinned by ConsumingRiteTests.HollowCoin_IsUntyped_SoNothingResistsIt :372). n=3 → round(3×4.45) = **13**.
- **ApplyPayoff (:43-50):** `if (res.Consumed.Count >= 3) target.ApplyEffect(new BrokenEffect(), ParentEntity, zone)`. With `Slots = 3` this means the Broken rider requires a **full three-mark cash-out** — an all-or-nothing threshold, unlike every other rite's `> 0` gate. `BrokenEffect(int duration = DURATION_INDEFINITE)`.
- **⚠ TUNING COMMENT IS LOAD-BEARING AND WAS ALREADY WRONG ONCE** (:26-34): "That table pays 0.4 per mark against a matched table's 0.5-0.75 … (It shipped at 0.5, which TIED three matched entries — Electric/Frozen, Heat/Frozen, Acid/Wet — and made this comment false for them. Caught by a cold-eye audit.)" The **identical comment block is duplicated verbatim** in SunderingWordMutation.cs:22-30 and BloodletterLedgerMutation.cs:23-31. In the port it should live once on the base or in the JSON description, not three times.
- **Targeting:** `Shape => SingleTarget` (:36), `Range => 4` (:37) → DirectionLine + FirstCreatureInLine.
- **Ink:** 1 charge via the base.
- **Overridden hooks:** `ApplyPayoff` (:43-50), `CastMessage` (:52-58). No `OnCastResolved`.
- **Messages:** marks>0 → `"<name> turns the hollow coin over — N mark(s) called in at once!"`; cold → `"<name> turns the hollow coin over. Nothing is owed."` (:54-57).
- **Diag:** base-supplied; filed under the **victim** (SingleTarget).
- **⚡ BROKEN by F17? YES.** SingleTarget is directional → base guard trips.
- **Cooldown:** 50 (:38) — the longest in the game. Tooltip agrees (GrimoireTooltipData.cs:200).
- **⚠ UNDOCUMENTED QUIRK the port should decide about:** with 3 slots against a 5-entry table, **which 3 statuses get spent is decided by `StatusEffectsPart._effects` insertion order** (ResonanceSystem.Preview iterates `GetAllEffects()` at :188-215, and `GetAllEffects()` returns the live list unsorted — StatusEffectsPart.cs:291). Since all five "Any" entries pay 0.4 the damage is order-independent *today*, but the surplus statuses go to `Declined{reason:"no_slots_left"}` and the player has no control over which survive for a follow-up. `ConsumingRiteHypothesisTests.Hypothesis_ATwoSlotRite_LeavesTheThirdStatusForTheFollowUp` (:286) pins this as intended behaviour, not a bug.
- **Port target:** mechanical, no new base hooks.


## SunderingWordMutation

FILE: Assets/Scripts/Gameplay/Mutations/SunderingWordMutation.cs (56 lines). **Extends `ConsumingRiteBase` (:14).**

- **Consumes:** `Element => "Any"` (:31) — wildcard table, all entries 0.4. `Slots => 2` (:35). n=2 → ×2.80.
- **Does:** debuff-first radius. `BaseDamage => 4` (:36), `DamageAttribute => ""` (:37, untyped). n=0 → 4; n=2 → round(4×2.8) = **11**.
- **ApplyPayoff (:39-47):** `if (res.Consumed.Count > 0) { target.ApplyEffect(new BrokenEffect(), ParentEntity, zone); target.ApplyEffect(new WeakenedEffect(), ParentEntity, zone); }` — the only rite that applies **two** effects unconditionally-on-marks. `BrokenEffect(duration = DURATION_INDEFINITE)`, `WeakenedEffect(int strPenalty = 2, int duration = 3)` — **note Weakened defaults to only 3 turns**, so the "leaves everything nearby less than it was" fiction is mostly the indefinite Broken.
- **Targeting:** `Shape => RiteShape.Radius` (:32), `Range => 2` (:33) → `SelfCentered` + `GetCreaturesInRadius(caster pos, 2, exclude: caster)`. Chebyshev, no occlusion.
- **Ink:** 1 charge via the base.
- **Overridden hooks:** `ApplyPayoff` (:39-47), `CastMessage` (:49-54). No `OnCastResolved`.
- **Messages:** `"<name> speaks the sundering word over N target(s)" + (marks>0 ? " — N unmade!" : ", and nothing answers.")` (:51-53). Note the marks>0 branch reuses `marks` for the "unmade" count, which reads as a target count but is a mark count — a cosmetic ambiguity worth fixing in the port.
- **Diag:** base-supplied; filed under **caster** (Radius).
- **✅ BROKEN by F17? NO — the second of the two surviving ConsumingRiteBase rites.** Same two reasons as Verdigris Bloom: `SelfCentered` never enters AwaitingDirection (InputHandler.cs:3052-3055), and the base's direction guard exempts Radius (ConsumingRiteBase.cs:115).
- **Cooldown:** 45 (:34); tooltip agrees (GrimoireTooltipData.cs:207).
- **Duplicated comment:** the wildcard-table tuning note at :22-30 is verbatim-identical to HollowCoinMutation.cs:26-34 and BloodletterLedgerMutation.cs:23-31.
- **Counter-check pinned:** ConsumingRiteTests.SunderingWord_RiderGuard_HoldsOnAColdCast (:587) — a cold cast applies neither Broken nor Weakened.
- **Port target:** mechanical, no new base hooks.


## BloodletterLedgerMutation

FILE: Assets/Scripts/Gameplay/Mutations/BloodletterLedgerMutation.cs (92 lines). **Extends `ConsumingRiteBase` (:15). The only rite that overrides `OnCastResolved` — and the reason that hook exists.**

- **Consumes:** `Element => "Any"` (:32) — wildcard, 0.4/mark. `Slots => 2` (:36). n=2 → ×2.80.
- **Does:** two things. **Debit** (target side, `ApplyPayoff` :40-57): `if (res.Consumed.Count == 0) return;` then `BleedingEffect(saveTarget: 14 + 4 * res.Consumed.Count, damageDice: res.Consumed.Count >= 2 ? "1d6" : "1d4")`. n=1 → save 18 / 1d4; n=2 → save 22 / 1d6. **Credit** (caster side, `OnCastResolved` :71-82): `heal = 4 * totalMarks`, `hp.BaseValue = Math.Min(hp.Max, hp.BaseValue + heal)`, plus `"<name> balances the ledger and closes N of their own wounds."`.
- **⚠ VERIFICATION-SWEEP CORRECTION preserved in-source (:45-51):** `BleedingEffect` has **no duration** — `Duration = DURATION_INDEFINITE` (BleedingEffect.cs:22) and recovery is a Toughness save that gets 1 easier per turn. So marks scale the only two knobs it has: `saveTarget` and `damageDice`. Ctor is `BleedingEffect(int saveTarget = 15, string damageDice = "1d2", System.Random rng = null)` — **the rng argument is omitted, so the bleed's saves are wall-clock seeded.** The port has `ctx.Rng` available; thread it.
- **⚠ THE BUG THAT CREATED `OnCastResolved` (:59-70):** the heal originally lived in `ApplyPayoff`, which the base skips when the target dies (ConsumingRiteBase.cs:158-159) — so "the better your setup, the more likely the sustain silently vanished, exactly inverting the design." Pinned by ConsumingRiteTests.BloodletterLedger_StillPays_WhenItsOwnDamageKills (:437). **Any port that collapses the two hooks re-introduces this bug.**
- **Damage:** `BaseDamage => 4` (:37), `DamageAttribute => ""` (:38, untyped). n=0 → 4; n=2 → round(4×2.8) = **11**.
- **Targeting:** `Shape => SingleTarget` (:33), `Range => 5` (:34) → DirectionLine + FirstCreatureInLine.
- **Ink:** 1 charge via the base.
- **Overridden hooks:** the only rite with **three** — `ApplyPayoff` (:40-57), `OnCastResolved` (:71-82), `CastMessage` (:84-90).
- **Messages:** heal line (:80-81, emitted from OnCastResolved, so it prints BEFORE the cast message — the base calls OnCastResolved at :164 and CastMessage at :184). Cast message: marks>0 → `"<name> writes N mark(s) into the bloodletter's ledger!"`; cold → `"<name> opens the ledger. The page stays blank."` (:86-89).
- **Diag:** base-supplied; filed under the **victim** (SingleTarget). **The heal amount is NOT in any diag payload** — `OnCastResolved` emits nothing. A player asking "how much did the ledger pay me?" can only read the message log. Worth fixing in the port.
- **⚡ BROKEN by F17? YES.** SingleTarget is directional → base guard trips.
- **Cooldown:** 40 (:35); tooltip agrees (GrimoireTooltipData.cs:214).
- **Anti-exploit pins:** ConsumingRiteAdversarialTests.Adversarial_LedgerCannotBeFarmedForInfiniteHealing (:212) and Adversarial_LedgerCannotHealAboveMaximum (:230). The clamp is `Math.Min(hp.Max, …)` at :79; the farm gate is ink + the 40-turn cooldown + "cold cast heals nothing".
- **Duplicated comment:** wildcard tuning note at :23-31, verbatim-identical to the other two "Any" rites.
- **Port target:** mechanical, but it is the **canary** — port it with its `OnCastResolved` test green before touching the others, because it is the one rite that proves the survivor-gate distinction survived the move.


## VERDICT — do the Rites deserve their own tree, or should they distribute into the elemental trees?

**Recommendation: their own tree, but gated by grimoire acquisition rather than by skill points. Do not distribute.** The argument from the actual mechanics, not the fiction:

**1. The distinguishing mechanic is CONSUMPTION, not element.** ResonanceSystem is the only system in the game that removes statuses as a cost (ResonanceSystem.cs:229-283, "This is the only place a status is spent"). Every skill-tree active applies statuses; no non-rite skill consumes one. The codebase says so in production comments, not just docs: Pyromancy_FlameJet.cs:16-21 — "A skill spell PRIMES… It never reads a status to spend it — that is a rite's job (SM7). Note the tree's older Pyromancy_Pyroclasm DOES consume Burning; it predates this design and is **grandfathered, not a precedent**." Distributing the rites into elemental trees deliberately destroys the boundary that comment was written to defend.

**2. The element split doesn't map cleanly anyway.** Electric ×3 (StormAnvil, HangingBolt, Fulmination) → Galvanism. Heat ×2 (RenderedSteam, ScaldingVeil) → Pyromancy. Cold ×2 (ShatteredRime, StillHeart) → Cryomancy. Acid ×1 (VerdigrisBloom) → Corrosion. **Any ×3** (HollowCoin, SunderingWord, BloodletterLedger) → belong to no element and would land in Spellcraft. Hydromancy gets zero. A distribution that leaves one tree empty, one tree with a single node, and a quarter of the feature in a catch-all is not a distribution — it's a scatter.

**3. The cost model is per-rite, not per-tree, and would become inconsistent inside trees.** All eleven cost 1 ink charge (`GrimoireInk.FindInked` → `TrySpend`, ConsumingRiteBase.cs:131-138). Every other skill active costs a cooldown only. Put Shattered Rime next to Cryomancy_RimeGrip and one node in the tree silently requires an inventory item the other six don't — the tree stops teaching a coherent cost. Keeping them together means "everything in this tree costs ink" is a one-sentence rule.

**4. Acquisition is already structurally different, and the trees are SP-purchase surfaces.** Rites are found (11 grimoire blueprints across LootTables.json; `GrimoirePart.DoRead` at GrimoirePart.cs:52-86 teaches on read and does not consume the book). Skill trees are bought (BuySkillAction.cs, `Cost: 1` per node in every Skills JSON). Distributing rites into SP trees forces one of two bad outcomes: make them purchasable and delete the grimoire economy, or leave unpurchasable nodes sitting inside purchasable trees. **A Rites tree sidesteps this: the tree is a display/grouping construct, individual rites are granted by `SkillsPart.AddSkill` from the book.** This also answers the plan's open question 3 (Docs/MUTATIONS-TO-SKILLS-MIGRATION.md:113).

**5. The cooldown band is a shared design statement.** Rites: 25, 25, 30, 30, 35, 40, 40, 40, 45, 45, 50. Skill actives in the elemental trees cluster at 35 (Pyromancy_FlameJet.cs:33, Cryomancy_RimeGrip.cs:24) with Hibernate the outlier at 200. Eight of eleven rites sit at or above 40. That band is the "finisher, not opener" rhythm — legible as a tree, invisible as scattered nodes.

**6. The UI already groups them, and porting will destroy that unless it's deliberate.** All six `AddMyActivatedAbility` call sites pass `abilityClass: "Rites"` (ConsumingRiteBase.cs:81, StormAnvil:59, HangingBolt:46, RenderedSteam:46, ScaldingVeil:39, Fulmination:56). `ActivatedAbility.Class` is a live sort/group key in the M-key ability manager (AbilityManagerStateBuilder.cs:61, and the class docstring at :12-16 names Class as the default sort). `ActivatedAbilitySpec.Class` defaults to `"Skills"` (ActivatedAbilitySpec.cs:31) — **set it to `"Rites"` explicitly in every ported rite or all eleven collapse into the Skills group.**

**The honest counter-argument, and why it loses.** Distribution would create real build synergy: a Cryomancy player naturally wants Shattered Rime and Still Heart, and their resonance tables (Cold: Wet .75/Frozen .75) are exactly what Cryomancy_RimeGrip and Cryomancy_Frostbind set up. That synergy is genuine — but it is already expressed through the *status table*, which is content, not through tree membership. A Cryomancy player who finds the Shattered Rime grimoire gets the synergy whether the rite lives in Cryomancy.json or Rites.json. Tree membership would add nothing except an SP-purchase path the rites explicitly don't want.

**Concrete shape:** `Assets/Resources/Content/Data/Skills/Rites.json`, root `RitesSkill` (`Class: "RitesSkill"`), 11 powers `Rites_StormAnvil` … `Rites_BloodletterLedger`, each with `Cost: 0` (or a `Grantable: true` flag if BuySkillAction needs one to refuse purchase — check BuySkillAction.cs before assuming Cost 0 hides a node). Descriptions lift straight from GrimoireTooltipData.cs:140-216 — **fix the two swapped cooldowns while you're there** (Rendered Steam is 35, not 30; Scalding Veil is 30, not 35).

# Part 3
======================================================================

> **NOTE:** **The plan's F12 undercounts and mis-frames.** It says "47 reference mutations, 73 reference skills" and infers "most mutation tests are behavioural and port to skill tests." The 47 is a case-sensitive grep and is correct as a raw number, but only **26 of those 47 files actually break**, and of the ~245 mutation-exercising test methods, roughly **78 are infrastructure that dies with zero replacement** — about a third, not "most." Conversely the risk sits in files the plan never names: farming, the ability-manager, and the test harness.

> **NOTE:** **BIGGEST SINGLE HAZARD: `MutationSystemTests.cs` is 24% not-a-mutation-test.** Lines 55–427 contain 22 `ActivatedAbilitiesPart` tests covering slot binding, cooldown ticking, legacy-assignment migration, and overflow handling. `ActivatedAbilitiesPart` **survives the migration** (skills declare abilities through it). Deleting the file wholesale in M4 silently removes 22 tests from live hotbar infrastructure with no compile error and no failing test to warn you. Move them to `Assets/Tests/EditMode/Gameplay/Abilities/ActivatedAbilitiesPartTests.cs` as the very first action of M4.

> **NOTE:** **The plan is silent on farming, and farming is a hard dependency.** `ConjureRainMutation` is the crop-watering mechanism. 54 test methods live in the three farming files, ~24 of them mutation-bearing, and `FarmingAuditFixTests` pins two production APIs the migration must replace: `GrimoireTooltipData.IsGrimoireMutation` (gates grimoire-picker visibility; 4 production consumers in `InventoryUI.cs` alone) and `GrimoirePart` field-copying on grimoire duplication. Add a farming line item to M3.

> **NOTE:** **`MutationDamageHelpers` cannot be deleted — the skill system calls it.** `Assets/Scripts/Gameplay/Skills/BaseSkillPart.cs:201`, `SkillEventDispatcher.cs:170`, and `SpellcraftSkill.cs:30,35` all reference it. M4's bullet "`MutationDamageHelpers` (fold into skill helpers)" is therefore a *rename+move on the critical path*, not a cleanup — and it must land before or with any mutation deletion, or the skill assembly breaks.

> **NOTE:** **One asmdef means M4 is all-or-nothing.** `Assets/Tests/EditMode/EditModeTests.asmdef` is the only test assembly (no PlayMode assembly exists). A single dangling `MutationsPart` reference yields zero test results, not 26 failures — so "tests still green" is not observable mid-migration. Fix all 26 files in the same commit as the deletion. Verify per MEMORY.md's `unity-compile-verification` ladder (grep Editor.log for `error CS`; `read_console` misses compile errors; a frozen test count means a stale assembly).

> **NOTE:** **Three smoke tests will stay green while doing nothing.** `ScenarioCustomSmokeTests` L54, L93, L126 combine `LogAssert.ignoreFailingMessages = true` with `Assert.DoesNotThrow`, over scenarios whose only real work is `PlayerBuilder.AddMutation("CalmMutation")` — which is fail-soft (`PlayerBuilder.cs:153,158`). After M4 the scenarios become inert and the tests never notice. The file's own comment at L56 predicts this. Any post-M3 verification that relies on these is worthless; convert them to positive `HasSkill` assertions.

> **NOTE:** **Two `Assembly.GetTypes()` scans are vacuous-loop risks** (`ConsumingRiteTests.cs:533` and `:677`) — the only reflection scans in the whole EditMode suite. If the ported base class doesn't match the filter, the loops execute zero iterations and pass. Every other rite enumeration uses an explicit array (e.g. `ConsumingRiteAdversarialTests.cs:443-448`) which fails loudly. On port, add a `Assert.Greater(count, 0)` precondition.

> **NOTE:** **Do not global-rename `Mutation`.** `PenetrationDiagTests.cs` (L325-386) asserts on the diag kind `"PreDamageMutation"`, emitted at `CombatSystem.cs:898`, meaning "a BeforeTakeDamage listener changed the amount." It is unrelated to the mutation system and is a live diag contract. Roughly 31 further files match only on "mutation" as engineering prose (state mutation, mutation-resistance testing). M5's prose sweep must be manual.

> **NOTE:** **The Rites are the largest and best-tested block, and they are all mutations.** 88 test methods across five files under `Gameplay/Magic/` — more than `GrimoireSpellTests` and `ProjectileMutationTests` combined — none of which look like mutation tests from their paths. `ConsumingRiteBase : BaseMutation` (`ConsumingRiteBase.cs:26`) is the only thing tying them in. M2 is the single highest-volume port and its adversarial + hypothesis files (29 tests) encode ink-economy invariants that exist nowhere else in prose or docs.

> **NOTE:** **Level-scaling is not quite the non-issue F3 claims, on the test side.** F3 is right that only 8 of 43 classes read `Level`. But two *tests* assert level scaling on reachable powers: `MutationSystemTests.FlamingHands_Cast_HigherLevelMoreDamage` (L1662) and `CalmMutationTests.CalmMutation_LevelScalesDuration` (L64, table-driven 3→70/5→90/10→140). Under the flat port both must be deliberately replaced by flat pins and recorded in the divergences table — not quietly dropped.

> **NOTE:** **The port has an FX-coverage gap.** `ProjectileMutationTests` calls `AsciiFxBus.Clear()` in Setup (L13) and asserts projectile/beam FX emission (L57, L94). No skill test file in `Gameplay/Skills/` touches `AsciiFxBus` at all. If those assertions are not carried over, the 29 ported powers ship with zero visual-emission test coverage.

> **NOTE:** **Honesty bound on my numbers.** Test-method counts are derived from a regex over `[Test]/[TestCase]/[TestCaseSource]/[UnityTest]` attributes followed by a `void`/`IEnumerator` declaration; `[TestCase]`-parameterised methods count once (6730 methods vs 6837 attribute rows). The per-test port/die classifications inside `MutationSystemTests.cs` come from reading the section banners and every test name, and the line ranges are verified — but I did not read all 94 bodies, so a handful near the FlamingHands/TurnManager boundary could shift by one or two between the 'port' and 'die' buckets. The ~245 semantic-count is a considered estimate with per-file evidence; the 489 compile-break count and the 26-file list are exact.


## CENSUS — the three grep tiers (correct the plan's F12)

`grep -rl -i --include='*.cs' mutation Assets/Tests/` = **78 files**. `grep -rl --include='*.cs' Mutation` (capital M) = **47 files** — this is the number in `Docs/MUTATIONS-TO-SKILLS-MIGRATION.md:31` (F12). The 31-file delta is pure prose ("state mutation", "mutation-resistance testing", "iterator-vs-mutation") and touches nothing.

Of the 47 capital-M files, only **26 contain a hard type/member reference** that breaks compilation when the mutation system is deleted. The other 21 match only on test-method names (`*_FailsWithoutMutation`, `*_NoMutation`), on the unrelated diag kind `PreDamageMutation`, or on string literals/comments.

Skill side: `grep -rl --include='*.cs' Skill Assets/Tests/` = **73 files** (matches the plan's F12 exactly), containing **914 test methods**. `Assets/Tests/EditMode/Gameplay/Skills/` alone = **50 files / 616 test methods**.

Whole suite: **6730 distinct test methods** in EditMode (6837 counting `[TestCase]` rows separately). 22 files reference BOTH systems.


## BLAST-RADIUS HAZARD — single assembly definition

`Assets/Tests/EditMode/EditModeTests.asmdef` is the **only** asmdef under `Assets/Tests/` (verified: `find Assets/Tests -name '*.asmdef'` returns exactly one). There is no PlayMode test assembly at all.

Consequence: all 6730 EditMode tests compile into one DLL. A single unresolved `MutationsPart` reference in any one of the 26 hard-reference files makes the **entire suite fail to build** — you get zero test results, not 26 failures. M4 ("delete the mutation system") therefore **cannot be landed as a partial commit**; all 26 files must be fixed in the same commit as the deletion, or the deletion must be split so that each file's fix precedes it.

Practical ordering: do M1/M2/M3 (additive), then in M4 fix all 26 test files and delete production classes in one atomic change. Verify with `refresh_unity mode=force` + grep Editor.log for `error CS` (per MEMORY.md `unity-compile-verification`) — `read_console` misses compile errors.


## Assets/Tests/EditMode/Gameplay/Mutations/MutationSystemTests.cs

**94 tests / 2021 lines / 252 hard mutation refs. Classification: MIXED — and it is the single most mis-classified file in the migration.**

The file is three files wearing a trenchcoat. Exact split by line range:

**(a) SURVIVES UNTOUCHED — 22 tests, lines 55–427.** Every `ActivatedAbility*` / `ActivatedAbilitiesPart_*` test. `ActivatedAbilitiesPart` is **not** being deleted (skills declare abilities through it — see `SkillActivatedAbilityTests.cs`). These pin: `IsUsable` vs cooldown (L55–84), `AddAbility` returns Guid (L85), `GetAbilityBySlot` ordering (L111), `RemoveAbility` removes from both list and slot map (L127, L312), `CooldownAbility` (L150), `TickCooldowns` floor-at-zero (L166, L187), auto-bind to first empty slot (L205), overflow stays in list without a slot (L222), `AssignAbilityToSlot` move/clear/out-of-range/unknown-id (L245–311), `MigrateLegacyAssignments` backfill / leave-explicit-alone / normalize-short-array (L347–414), and `EndTurn` ticks cooldowns (L415). **ACTION: move these 22 verbatim into a new `Assets/Tests/EditMode/Gameplay/Abilities/ActivatedAbilitiesPartTests.cs` BEFORE deleting this file.** Only one needs an edit: `ActivatedAbilitiesPart_AddAbility_ReturnsGuid` passes the literal `"Physical Mutations"` at L92 — change to `"Skills"`. If you delete the file wholesale you silently lose 22 tests covering live hotbar/ability infrastructure.

**(b) PORT TO SKILLS — 9 tests.** FlamingHands block L1586–1766 (8): `Mutate_RegistersAbility` (L1587), `Unmutate_RemovesAbility` (L1600), `Cast_DamagesCreaturesInCell` (L1614), `Cast_PutsOnCooldown` (L1640), `Cast_HigherLevelMoreDamage` (L1662 — **the only level-scaling test among the 29 reachable powers; per F3 the port is flat, so this test does not port, it DIES and should be replaced by a flat-damage pin**), `Cast_EmptyCell_LogsMessage` (L1700), `CommandEvent_TriggersCast` (L1720), `Cast_CanKillCreature` (L1743); plus `Integration_FlamingHands_CooldownTicksOverTurns` (L1989) and `TurnManager_EndTurn_FiresEndTurnEvent` (L1884, survives as-is).

**(c) DIES — 62 tests.** BaseMutation level math L640–726 (7: `Mutate_SetsBaseLevel`, `DefaultLevel_IsOne`, `Phase7Defaults_AreNoOp`, `GetMutationCapForLevel_UsesQudFormula`, `CalcLevel_RespectsLevelCap`, `RapidLevel_IncreasesEffectiveLevel`, `CanIncreaseLevel_StopsAtMaxLevel`) — F4 says no concrete mutation implements `IRankedMutation`, so this math governs nothing. `MutationRegistry_InitializeFromJson` (L727). MutationsPart CRUD L750–922 (12). ExtraArmPrototype L923–989 (3). LevelMutation (L990). Entity MP L1003–1045 (3). SpendMP L1046–1086 (2). RandomBuy L1087–1230 (6). Esper/Chimera L1231–1300 (2). Genome L1301–1425 (4). MutationMods L1426–1468 (3). DuplicateRanked (L1469). GetMutatePool (L1495). SyncMutationLevels L1527–1585 (3). Telepathy L1767–1821 (4).
